An excerpt from the [Meshari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Meshari) *(The Missal)* written by [Gjon Buzuku](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gjon_Buzuku) (1555)

The cultural renaissance was first of all expressed through the development of the Albanian language in the area of church texts and publications. The Protestant reforms invigorated hopes for the development of the local language and literary tradition, when cleric [Gjon Buzuku](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gjon_Buzuku) translated the [Catholic liturgy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_liturgy) into Albanian, trying to do for Albanian what [Martin Luther](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martin_Luther) had done for German. [Meshari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Meshari) (*The Missal*) written by [Gjon Buzuku](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gjon_Buzuku) was published in 1555 and is considered one of the first literary works of written Albanian during the Middle Ages. The refined level of the language and the stabilised orthography must be the result of an earlier tradition of written Albanian, a tradition that is not well understood. However, there is some fragmented evidence, pre-dating Buzuku, which indicates that Albanian was written from at least the 14th century.&#x20;



[Parashqevi Qiriazi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Parashqevi_Qiriazi) – teacher and feminist (1880–1970)

Albanian writings from these centuries must not have been religious texts only, but historical chronicles too. They are mentioned by the humanist [Marin Barleti](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marin_Barleti), who in his book [Siege of Shkodër](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siege_of_Shkodra_\(1478\)#The_Book) (*Rrethimi i Shkodrës*) from 1504, confirms that he leafed through such chronicles written in the language of the people (*in vernacula lingua*) as well as his famous biography of [Skanderbeg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Skanderbeg) [Historia de vita et gestis Scanderbegi Epirotarum principis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marin_Barleti) (*History of Skanderbeg*) from 1508. The *History of Skanderbeg* is still the foundation of Skanderbeg studies and is considered an Albanian cultural treasure, vital to the formation of Albanian national self-consciousness.&#x20;

During the 16th and the 17th centuries, the [catechism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catechism) (*E mbësuame krishterë*) (Christian Teachings) from 1592 written by [Lekë Matrënga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lek%C3%AB_Matr%C3%ABnga), (*Doktrina e krishterë*) (The Christian Doctrine) from 1618 and (*Rituale romanum*) 1621 by [Pjetër Budi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pjet%C3%ABr_Budi), the first writer of original Albanian [prose](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prose) and poetry, an [apology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Remorse) for George Castriot (1636) by [Frang Bardhi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frang_Bardhi), who also published a dictionary and [folklore](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Folklore) creations, the theological-philosophical treaty *[Cuneus Prophetarum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cuneus_Prophetarum)* (The Band of Prophets) (1685) by [Pjetër Bogdani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pjet%C3%ABr_Bogdani), the most universal personality of Albanian [Middle Ages](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_Ages), were published in [Albanian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_language). The most famous Albanian writer in the 20th and 21st century is probably [Ismail Kadare](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ismail_Kadare).&#x20;

### Cuisine

Throughout the centuries, [Albanian cuisine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_cuisine) has been widely influenced by Albanian culture, geography and history, and as such, different parts of the country enjoy specific [regional cuisines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Regional_cuisine). Cooking traditions especially vary between the north and the south, owing to differing topography and climate that essentially contribute to the excellent growth conditions for a wide array of herbs, fruits, and vegetables.[^cite_note-386]&#x20;

Albanians produce and use many varieties of fruits such as lemons, oranges, figs, and most notably, olives, which are perhaps the most important element of Albanian cooking. Spices and other herbs such as basil, lavender, mint, oregano, rosemary, and thyme are widely used, as are vegetables such as garlic, onions, peppers, potatoes, tomatoes, as well as legumes of all types.&#x20;

With a coastline along the Adriatic and Ionian in the Mediterranean Sea, fish, crustaceans, and seafood are a popular and an integral part of the diet. Otherwise, lamb is the traditional meat for different holidays and religious festivals for both Christians and Muslims, although poultry, beef, and pork are also in plentiful supply.&#x20;

[Tavë kosi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tav%C3%AB_kosi) ("[soured milk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soured_milk) [casserole](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Casserole)") is the national dish, consisting of lamb and rice baked under a thick, tart veil of yoghurt. [Fërgesë](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/F%C3%ABrges%C3%AB) is another national dish, made up of peppers, tomatoes, and cottage cheese. Pite is also popular, a baked pastry with a filling of a mixture of spinach and gjizë (curd) or mish (ground meat).&#x20;

Petulla, a traditional fried dough, is also a popular speciality, and is served with powdered sugar or feta cheese and different sorts of fruit jams. [Flia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flia) consists of multiple crêpe-like layers brushed with cream and served with sour cream. [Krofne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Krofne), similar to [Berliner doughnuts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Berliner_\(doughnut\)), are filled with jam, or chocolate and often eaten during winter.&#x20;

Coffee is an integral part of the lifestyle, with more coffee houses per capita than any other country in the world.[^cite_note-387] Tea is also enjoyed both at home or outside at cafés, bars, or restaurants. Çaj Mali ([Sideritis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sideritis) tea) is beloved and a part of the daily routine for most Albanians. It is cultivated across Southern Albania and noted for its medicinal properties. [Black tea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_tea) is also popular. [Albanian wine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_wine) has been cultivated [for thousands of years](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_World_wine).[^cite_note-388][^cite_note-389]&#x20;

- Bukë misri (cornbread) is a staple on the Albanian table.

  Bukë misri ([cornbread](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cornbread)) is a staple on the Albanian table.

- Speca të fërguar (roasted peppers) served with pite, a traditional and prominent layered Albanian pie

  Speca të fërguar (roasted [peppers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Capsicum)) served with [pite](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pite_\(food\)), a traditional and prominent layered Albanian pie

### Sports

See also: [Albania at the Olympics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albania_at_the_Olympics) and [Albania at the Mediterranean Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albania_at_the_Mediterranean_Games)



[Arena Kombëtare](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arena_Komb%C3%ABtare) in central Tirana

Albania participated at the [Olympic Games in 1972](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1972_Summer_Olympics) for the first time. The country made their Winter Olympic Games debut in [2006](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winter_Olympics_2006). Albania missed the next four games, two of them due to the 1980 and 1984 boycotts, but returned for the 1992 games in [Barcelona](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1992_Olympic_Winter_Games). Since then, Albania have participated in all games. Albania normally competes in events that include swimming, athletics, weightlifting, shooting and wrestling. The country have been represented by the [National Olympic Committee of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Olympic_Committee_of_Albania) since 1972. The nation has participated at the [Mediterranean Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_Games) since the games of [1987](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1987_Mediterranean_Games) in Syria. The Albanian athletes have won a total of 43 (8 gold, 17 silver and 18 bronze) medals from [1987](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1987_Mediterranean_Games) to [2013](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_Mediterranean_Games).&#x20;

Popular sports include [football](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Football_in_Albania), weightlifting, [basketball](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albania_national_basketball_team), volleyball, tennis, swimming, [rugby union](25405.md) and gymnastics. Football is by far the most popular sport and is governed by the [Football Association of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Football_Association_of_Albania) ([Albanian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_language): *Federata Shqiptare e Futbollit*, F.SH.F.), which has membership in [FIFA](11049.md) and [UEFA](32332.md). The [Albania national football team](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albania_national_football_team), ranking 51st in the [World](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_World_Rankings) in 2017 (highest 22nd on 22 August 2015) have won the [1946 Balkan Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1946_Balkan_Cup) and the [Malta Rothmans International Tournament 2000](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rothmans_International_Tournament_2000), but had never participated in any major UEFA or FIFA tournament, until [UEFA Euro 2016](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UEFA_Euro_2016), Albania's first ever appearance at the continental tournament and at a major men's football tournament. Albania scored their first ever goal in a major tournament and secured their first ever win in European Championship when they beat [Romania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romania_national_football_team) by 1–0 in a [UEFA Euro 2016](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UEFA_Euro_2016) match on 19 June 2016.[^cite_note-390][^cite_note-391] The most successful football clubs are [Skënderbeu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/KF_Sk%C3%ABnderbeu_Kor%C3%A7%C3%AB), [KF Tirana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/KF_Tirana), [Dinamo Tirana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FK_Dinamo_Tirana), [Partizani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FK_Partizani_Tirana) and [Vllaznia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/KF_Vllaznia_Shkod%C3%ABr).&#x20;

Weightlifting is one of the most successful individual sports, with the national team winning medals at the [European Weightlifting Championships](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Weightlifting_Championships) and the rest international competitions. Albanian weightlifters have won a total of 16 medals at the [European Championships](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Weightlifting_Championships) with one of them being gold, seven silver and eight bronze. In the [World Weightlifting Championships](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Weightlifting_Championships), the Albanian weightlifting team has won in [1972](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1972_World_Weightlifting_Championships) a gold, in [2002](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2002_World_Weightlifting_Championships) a silver, and in [2011](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_World_Weightlifting_Championships) a bronze medal.&#x20;

Situated on the shores of Lake Ohrid, the town of Ohrid is one of the oldest human settlements in Europe; Lake Ohrid is a superlative natural phenomenon, providing refuge for numerous endemic and relict freshwater species of flora and fauna dating from the tertiary period. As a deep and ancient lake of tectonic origin, Lake Ohrid has existed continuously for approximately two to three million years.

[^cite_note-Shkurtaj2017-327]: &#x20;Gjovalin Shkurtaj (2017). *Urgjenca gjuhësore: -huazime të zëvendësueshme me fjalë shqipe- : (fjalorth)*. Naimi. pp. 15–16. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\)) [9789928234049](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special\:BookSources/9789928234049). Sic u permend me lart, per shkak te shkaqeve kulturore dhe ekonomike, trendet e mesimit te gjuheve nga te rinjte (grupmosha deri ne 25 vjec) ndryshojne. Keto trende jane percaktues i nje sere fenomeneve shoqerore, sic do te shohim me tej. Keshtu nga viti 2000 e ketej, gjuha angleze, gjermane dhe ajo turke kane pasur nje rritje te interest. Gjuha italiane, por edhe ajo franceze kane pasur nje stabilitet, pra as rritje dhe as ulje te interesit te pergjithshem nga ana e grupmoshes te siperpermendur. Vihet re se gjuha greke ka pesuar nje renie te forte te interesit. Ne fakt, shumica e interesit ka rene per kete gjuhe. Arsyet per kete gjuhe specifike do ti trajtojme me tej ne kapitulin e trete.
